{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport scipy.stats as st\nvla = [80.0, 79.0, 78.5, 77.0, 80.5, 80.0, 79.5, 78.5]\nadp = [82.0, 80.0, 80.5, 80.0, 80.0, 81.5, 81.0, 82.0]\ndelta = [a-v for a,v in zip(adp,vla)]\nprint('deltas:', delta)\nprint('mean Δ:', sum(delta)/len(delta))\nt,p = st.ttest_rel(adp, vla)\nprint(f'paired t-test: t={t:.4f}, p={p:.4f}')\nw,wp = st.wilcoxon(adp, vla)\nprint(f'Wilcoxon signed-rank: W={w:.4f}, p={wp:.4f}')\n# also recompute with s45 vla = 80.0 (alt duplicate)\nvla2 = [80.0, 79.0, 78.5, 80.0, 80.5, 80.0, 79.5, 78.5]\ndelta2 = [a-v for a,v in zip(adp,vla2)]\nprint('alt deltas (s45=80):', delta2)\nt,p = st.ttest_rel(adp, vla2)\nprint(f'alt paired t-test: t={t:.4f}, p={p:.4f}')\nw,wp = st.wilcoxon(adp, vla2)\nprint(f'alt Wilcoxon: W={w:.4f}, p={wp:.4f}')\n\"\n",
  "description": "Compute paired stats"
}

## Spatial 8-pair 完整原始数据表

**数据源**：Registry 中所有 `eval-supp-*` clean 命名约定的 Spatial 实验（每对 n_rollouts=20, 10 tasks → 200 rollouts/seed）。
**Adaptive 配置**：mode=adaptive_conformal_crm, τ=0.25, K=2, max_correction_norm=0.2, CRM ckpt=crm_smolvla_3suite_v1。

```
seed | VLA-only SR | Adaptive SR | Δ      | VLA-only exp_id                     | Adaptive exp_id                       | 备注
s42  | 80.0%       | 82.0%       | +2.0pp | eval-supp-spatial-vla-only-s42      | eval-supp-adapt-tau025-spatial-s42-r2 | 干净；Adapt: r2 (r1 killed)
s43  | 79.0%       | 80.0%       | +1.0pp | eval-supp-spatial-vla-only-s43      | eval-supp-adapt-tau025-spatial-s43    | 干净
s44  | 78.5%       | 80.5%       | +2.0pp | eval-supp-spatial-vla-only-s44      | eval-supp-adapt-tau025-spatial-s44    | 干净
s45  | 77.0%       | 80.0%       | +3.0pp | eval-supp-spatial-vla-only-s45      | eval-supp-adapt-tau025-spatial-s45    | 干净；s45 VLA-only 有重复 run (80.0%, eval-supp-vla-only-spatial-s45 @ bjb2-28827)
s46  | 80.5%       | 80.0%       | -0.5pp | eval-supp-spatial-vla-only-s46      | eval-supp-spatial-adapt-tau025-s46    | 干净；Adapt 重复 (eval-supp-adapt-tau025-spatial-s46) killed
s47  | 80.0%       | 81.5%       | +1.5pp | eval-supp-vla-only-spatial-s47      | eval-supp-adapt-tau025-spatial-s47    | 干净；VLA-only 重复 (eval-supp-spatial-vla-only-s47=81.0%), Adapt 重复 (eval-supp-spatial-adapt-tau025-s47=79.0%)
s48  | 79.5%       | 81.0%       | +1.5pp | eval-supp-vla-only-spatial-s48      | eval-supp-adapt-tau025-spatial-s48    | 干净；status=PREPARING 但 conclusion=success
s49  | 78.5%       | 82.0%       | +3.5pp | eval-supp-vla-only-spatial-s49      | eval-supp-adapt-tau025-spatial-s49    | 干净；status=PREPARING 但 conclusion=success
```

**汇总**：mean Δ = +1.75pp，n=8
- **配对 t-test：t=4.0415，p=0.0049** ✓ 与 reviewer 数字完全一致
- **Wilcoxon signed-rank：W=1.0，p=0.0156**

**敏感性检查**（若 s45 用 VLA-only 重复值 80.0%）：
- mean Δ = +1.31pp，paired t=3.1202，p=0.0168；Wilcoxon W=1, p=0.0312
- 结论：仍显著（p<0.05），但 p=0.0049 这一具体数字依赖于 s45 选用 77.0% 那条 run

**Trigger Rate（Adaptive）**：s42=19.4%, s43=19.4%, s44=20.0%, s45=20.13%, s46=19.9%, s47=20.46%, s48=19.8%, s49=19.5%（mean ≈19.8%，与 D140 trigger↔效应大小相关性一致）

**服务器分布**：
- VLA-only：bjb1-18228 (s42), bjb2-40593 (s44), cqa1-32758 (s43, s45, s46, s47, s48, s49)
- Adaptive：bjb1-32546 (s42-s44), bjb2-28827 (s45, s48, s49), cqa1-32758 (s46, s47)

**已知数据备注**：
- s42 VLA-only：spec 命令缺 `--model pi05` flag，但 wall_clock 3.14h 与 pi05 一致（SmolVLA 应 ~30min）。`artifacts/_project/data/table1_8seed_final.md` 已标注此异常。若剔除 s42 → n=7，需重新计算。
- s48/s49 在 registry 中 status 字段未更新为 DONE（仍为 PREPARING/RUNNING），但 metrics + conclusion=success 已写入，结果有效。
- 所有数据来自 `eval-supp-*` 干净 pipeline；早期 `eval-pi05-vla-only-spatial-seed*` (SR≈30%) 和 `eval-vla-only-spatial-seed*` (SR≈30%) 等是早期 SmolVLA/不同配置的产物，**不应混入**此 8-pair。
